rating: 5
review_text: Got breakfast here on the last day of our trip, and it was great. Bonnie was our server and she took incredible care of us! The atmosphere is quite lively w/ music, and many servers hustling and bustling around, I found it quite charming! There’s 50s trivia on the menu which is an easy way to stay entertained & prompt conversation while waiting for food.

I am pescatarian and there are pes & vegetarian options available. My guess is they can modify to make dishes gluten-free as well, though I didn’t ask to confirm.

As far as the food goes, I got the Shrimp Benedict which was rich, creamy, and delicious. The eggs were poached to perfection and the shrimp were not overcooked at all. My partner got a Garden Omelette which she said was great, and my mother got the Classic Breakfast and ate every single bite. She said the grits were perfectly cooked as well. We all got coffee which we enjoyed, not too bitter, too weak, or too strong! Great cup :)

Would recommend this place. The staff we interacted with were very pleasant. Once again, Bonnie did great!

--- entity_reviews [row 4 of 5] ---
id: 04192b06-84bc-463a-99c1-9804123f9bc6
entity_slug: sunliner-diner-gulf-shores
reviewer_name: Lucas Ondak
rating: 5
body: Got breakfast here on the last day of our trip, and it was great. Bonnie was our server and she took incredible care of us! The atmosphere is quite lively w/ music, and many servers hustling and bustling around, I found it quite charming! There’s 50s trivia on the menu which is an easy way to stay entertained & prompt conversation while waiting for food.

I am pescatarian and there are pes & vegetarian options available. My guess is they can modify to make dishes gluten-free as well, though I didn’t ask to confirm.

As far as the food goes, I got the Shrimp Benedict which was rich, creamy, and delicious. The eggs were poached to perfection and the shrimp were not overcooked at all. My partner got a Garden Omelette which she said was great, and my mother got the Classic Breakfast and ate every single bite. She said the grits were perfectly cooked as well. We all got coffee which we enjoyed, not too bitter, too weak, or too strong! Great cup :)

Would recommend this place. The staff we interacted with were very pleasant. Once again, Bonnie did great!
